Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the ocean framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 543. The resulting operator achieves bound O(922/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 543 + O(1/n^922). Summing over all modes and applying the ocean convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
